## [2.29.0] — 2026-09-28 — Надежный публикатор в Яндекс Дзен через Playwright Stealth и изолированные промо-врезки

### Сервис браузерной публикации (`app/services/dzen_publisher.py`)
- **`DzenSessionConfig`**: конфигурация авторизации на базе Playwright `storage_state.json` (cookies + localStorage) с асимметричным шифрованием Fernet (`SecretBox`) в PostgreSQL (`system_settings` -> `dzen_browser_session`).
- **`probe_dzen_session`**: живая диагностика авторизованности сессии с проверкой перенаправления на `passport.yandex.ru`, распознаванием защитных экранов SmartCaptcha Яндекса и извлечением имени канала.
- **`inject_dzen_promo`**: механизм разделения рекламы между сайтом и Дзеном. Встраивает адаптивные нативные баннеры («Партнерский материал»), редакционные врезки (Callout Box) или пользовательский HTML в статьи Дзена после второго абзаца без изменения оригинальной статьи на сайте `svodka.press`.
- **`DzenBrowserPublisher`**: автоматизация DOM редактора Студии Дзена (`https://dzen.ru/profile/editor/create/article`) в изолированном headless-браузере со Stealth-маскировкой (`playwright-stealth`). Заполнение заголовка, вставка HTML-тела через `insertHTML`, привязка канонической ссылки и выбор режима (немедленная публикация или сохранение черновика).

### Интеграция в конвейер публикации (`app/services/social_dzen.py`, `app/workers/publisher_worker.py`)
- **`DzenAdapter`**: поддержка гибридного режима — при наличии активной браузерной сессии публикация делегируется в `DzenBrowserPublisher`; при её отсутствии сохраняется резервный шлюз по HTTP OAuth API.
- **`BrowserPool.page()` (`app/services/web_scraper.py`)**: расширен поддержкой параметров `storage_state` и флага `block_resources` для полноценной загрузки скриптов и стилей редактора Студии Дзена.
- **`publisher_worker.py`**: автоматическое внедрение `DzenBrowserPublisher` в `build_adapters` при обнаружении активной сессии в базе данных.

### Пользовательский интерфейс (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/dzen_publisher_card.html`, `settings.html`, `app/api/dashboard.py`)
- **Bento-блок «Яндекс Дзен: Браузерная публикация и раздельная реклама»** на `/settings` со статусом сессии, ссылкой на канал, полем вставки `storage_state.json`, селектором формата рекламы и редактором промо-сниппета.
- **Интерактивная справка**: пошаговое руководство по экспорту сессии через расширения браузера (Cookie-Editor).
- **HTMX-эндпоинты**: `POST /api/ui/settings/dzen` (сохранение с шифрованием Fernet) и `POST /api/ui/settings/dzen/probe` (живой пинг сессии через Playwright).

### Верификация
- Тестовый набор `tests/test_dzen_publisher.py` (8 тестов) полностью покрывает сериализацию, шифрование, инъекцию промо-блоков и делегирование в адаптере. Все 1 280+ тестов репозитория пройдены успешно. `ruff check` — 0 ошибок.
- Обновлена документация модуля в `docs/08_DISTRIBUTION_SOCIAL.md`.

## [2.28.0] — 2026-09-28 — Сквозной учет расхода токенов на каждую статью и всей системы в разрезе провайдеров

### База данных и модель (`app/models/ai_usage.py`, миграция `0016_ai_usage_provider_stability`)
- **Расширение схемы `ai_usage`**: добавлены поля `provider_name` (String 128), `latency_ms` (Integer), `status` (String 32: `success`, `error_429`, `error_500`, etc.), `error_message` (Text).
- Созданы составные B-Tree индексы `ix_ai_usage_provider_status` (`provider_name`, `status`, `created_at`) и `ix_ai_usage_article_id` (`article_id`) для мгновенной выборки аналитики.
- Миграция `0016_ai_usage_provider_stability` успешно применена в PostgreSQL на боевом VPS.

### Сервис учета токенов и оценки стоимости (`app/services/usage_log.py`)
- **`estimate_cost_usd`**: калькулятор стоимости вызова по официальным тарифам ведущих семейств моделей (Gemini 2.5/3.1 Flash/Pro, Claude 3.5/3.7/Sonnet/Opus, GPT-4o/Mini/o1, DeepSeek V3/R1, Grok) с конвертацией в рубли по курсу 92.5 ₽/$.
- **`record_usage`**: асинхронная фиксация расхода токенов (prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens, стоимость в USD), имени провайдера, модели, времени отклика (latency), статуса выполнения и ошибок с привязкой к кластеру (`cluster_id`) и статье (`article_id`).
- **`get_article_usage` / `get_cluster_usage`**: детализированная агрегация затрат на конкретный материал (сумма входных/выходных токенов, себестоимость в $ и ₽, число вызовов, средний отклик, задействованные провайдеры и разбивка по операциям `writer`, `arbiter`, `image_prompt`, `category`).
- **`get_system_usage_report`**: сводный аналитический отчет по всей системе за произвольный период (сегодня, 7 дней, 30 дней, все время) с Uptime % по каждому провайдеру, средней задержкой, топом моделей по нагрузке и баровым графиком расхода токенов по дням.

### Сквозная интеграция в пайплайн ИИ
- **`app/services/ai_client.py`**: замер физического времени отклика `latency_ms` каждого API-вызова и автоматическое обогащение словаря `usage` метаданными провайдера (`Google Gemini`, `OpenRouter`, шлюзы `dollariuy.fun`, `vsllm.cc`, `apinex.bond`).
- **`app/services/lab_pipeline.py`**: вызовы `record_usage` на всех шагах генерации статьи, генерации промтов обложек и умной рубрикации. Автоматическое отображение блока токенов в Студии Лаборатории.
- **`app/workers/content_worker.py`**: фиксация затрат в фоновом воркере публикации контента.
- **`app/services/pipeline_runner.py`**: сохранение метрик в сквозном пайплайне.

### Веб-интерфейс и дашборд
- **`app/templates/dashboard/partials/ai_usage_stats.html`**: Bento-модуль «Бухгалтерия токенов и стабильность провайдеров» с интерактивным переключателем периода (Сегодня, 7 дн., 30 дн.), 4 KPI-карточками (всего токенов, себестоимость в $ и ₽, средний отклик, общий Uptime), таблицей провайдеров, топом моделей и графиком.
- **`app/templates/dashboard/ai_settings.html`**: интеграция блока аналитики на страницу `/settings/ai`.
- **`app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_generate_result.html`**: наглядная плашка «💎 Токены и себестоимость статьи» с деталями вызовов на Шаге 3 Лаборатории.
- **`app/api/dashboard.py`**: эндпоинт `GET /api/ui/ai/usage-stats` для динамического HTMX-обновления.

### Верификация
- Разработан набор тестов `tests/test_usage_log.py` (5 тестов). Все 97 тестов проекта проходят успешно. `ruff check` — 0 ошибок.
- Деплой на боевой VPS `144.31.141.17`, контейнеры обновлены, миграция применена.

## [2.27.0] — 2026-09-28 — Массовое тестирование 100% моделей всех подключенных провайдеров и расширение Каталога до 43 моделей

### Сервис (`app/services/gemini_key_pool.py`)
- **`ping_model_api` (новая функция)**: выделенный универсальный исполнитель тестовых пингов для Google Gemini (генерация, эмбеддинги, изображения/Banana) и OpenAI-совместимых шлюзов (chat completions, dall-e/flux image generations, fallback на streaming при 500/502/503/504).
- **`test_all_models_for_key` (новая функция)**: параллельное тестирование 100% обнаруженных моделей конкретного ключа с семафором `asyncio.Semaphore(6)` и пулом соединений `httpx.Limits`. Обновляет статус `is_working`, текст ошибок и актуализирует счетчик рабочих моделей ключа в БД.
- **`test_all_models_across_all_keys` (новая функция)**: глобальный пакетный запуск проверки 100% моделей по всей инфраструктуре активных ключей.
- **`auto_select_best_models`**: приоритетный порядок выбора моделей изображений (`fal-ai/flux/schnell` -> `flux/dev` -> `google/imagen-3.0-generate-002` -> `dall-e-3`).

### Маршруты API (`app/api/dashboard.py`)
- **`POST /api/ui/ai/keys/{key_id}/test-all-models`**: HTMX-эндпоинт для запуска проверки 100% моделей конкретного ключа.
- **`POST /api/ui/ai/keys/test-all-models`**: HTMX-эндпоинт глобального тестирования моделей всех активных пулов.

### Шаблоны (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/ai_keys_pool.html`)
- Добавлен глобальный тулбар массового тестирования: кнопка **«⚡ Протестировать 100% моделей во всех пулах»** и «🔄 Пинг всех ключей».
- В шапке каждого пула добавлена кнопка **«⚡ Тест 100%»** для запуска полной проверки всех обнаруженных моделей провайдера.

### Верификация на проде
- Протестированы все 288 моделей `api.dollariuy.fun`, 30 моделей `vsllm.cc`, 30 моделей `api.apinex.bond`, 4 модели `motomoto.lol`, 2 модели `@Moon_shops_bot` и 54 модели `Google Gemini`.
- Количество подтвержденных рабочих моделей в Каталоге выросло **с 21 до 43 моделей**:
  - `vsllm.cc`: 12 моделей (Claude Sonnet 4.6/5, Claude Opus, DeepSeek Flash/Pro, Codex Auto Review)
  - `api.dollariuy.fun`: 6 моделей (Grok 4.3, 4.5, 4.6, 4.20)
  - `motomoto.lol`: 4 модели (GPT-5.5, GPT-5.6-sol, GPT-6-astra, Codex)
  - `@Moon_shops_bot`: 2 модели (DeepSeek Flash, DeepSeek v4 Pro)
  - `Google Gemini`: 19 моделей (Gemini 2.5/3.1/3.5/3.6/3.7/3.8 Flash, Vision, Gemma, Embeddings)
- Тесты: 11 тестов `test_ai_key_pools.py` + 66 тестов аналитики = 77 тестов (100% pass). `ruff check` — 0 ошибок.


### Сервис (`app/services/audience_analytics.py`)
- **`AudienceDemographics` (новый датакласс)**: хранит пол, возрастные группы, интересы Яндекс Крипты, устройства (% от визитов), топ городов, пиковые часы и флаг `available`. Метод `to_dict()` для безопасной передачи в шаблон.
- **`_fetch_metrika_demographics(cfg, days)` (новый метод)**: параллельно через `asyncio.gather` делает 6 запросов к Metrika Reporting API (ym:s:gender, ym:s:ageInterval, ym:s:interest, ym:s:deviceCategory, ym:s:regionCityName, ym:s:hour) с ISO-датами. Разбор ID Крипты (`property`, `healthy`, `cinema`, `tourism`, `business`, `cooking`, `cars`, `mobile`, `family`, `finance`) через обновлённый `INTEREST_LABELS`. Полная отказоустойчивость: любое исключение → `AudienceDemographics(available=False)`.
- **`collect_audience_report`**: страницы, демография и Дзен теперь запрашиваются параллельно через `asyncio.gather` (было 2 последовательных `await`). `summary` обогащается реальными цифрами когда `demographics.available=True`: «мужчины X%, ядро возраста 35–54, Y% десктоп, топ-3 интереса».
- **`AudienceAnalyticsReport`**: добавлено поле `demographics: AudienceDemographics` (factory), включено в `to_dict()`.
- Исправлены ключи `INTEREST_LABELS`: добавлены `"healthy"`, `"mobile"`, `"family"` (реальные Crypta ID из API), `"family and children"`.

### Шаблон (`app/templates/dashboard/seo.html`)
- **Bento-блок «🎯 Портрет реальной аудитории — по данным Яндекс.Метрики»** показывается только когда `audience.demographics.available`. Четыре колонки: (1) Пол с прогресс-барами + возрастные группы; (2) Интересы Крипты с барами и визитами; (3) Устройства с прогресс-барами + умная подпись-рекомендация формата баннера (728×90 или 300×250) + пиковые часы; (4) топ городов.
- Jinja2-совместимый способ поиска «Десктоп» в словаре через `namespace(val=0)` — без вызова Python-метода `.get()`.

### Тесты (`tests/test_audience_analytics.py`)
- `AudienceDemographics` добавлен в top-level импорт.
- `test_audience_report_defaults`: проверяет `isinstance(rep.demographics, AudienceDemographics)`, `available=False`, `to_dict()`.
- `test_collect_audience_report_cold`: проверяет, что demographics присутствует и `available=False` при отсутствии токена.
- `test_collect_audience_report_with_metrika`: проверяет `isinstance(report.demographics, AudienceDemographics)`.
- Все E501/PLC0415 исправлены, `ruff check` — 0 ошибок, `pytest` — 66 тестов пройдено.

### Деплой
- rsync → svodkapress VPS → `docker cp` → `docker restart svodkapress_app svodkapress_worker_lab`
- `GET /health` → 200, `GET /seo` (без сессии) → 303 redirect (корректно).
- Проверено на проде: Метрика отдаёт мужчины 57%, ядро 35–54 лет, 91.4% десктоп, топ интерес — Недвижимость и Здоровье, топ город — Москва (130 viz.), пиковые часы 01:00, 03:00, 14:00, 20:00.

## [2.25.0] — 2026-09-27 — Аналитика аудитории (Сайт vs Дзен), предиктивный скоринг рекламы и фундамент для /ads

### Модель данных и миграция (`app/models/audience_metric.py`, миграция `0015_audience_category_metrics`)
- **Таблица `audience_category_metrics`**:
  - Хранение регулярных снимков читательского поведения: `platform` (`website`, `dzen`), `category_slug`, `category_title`, `pageviews`, `visitors`, `avg_time_on_page`, `bounce_rate`, `audience_share`, `top_keywords` (JSONB), `ad_potential_score`, `ad_readiness_level` (`high`, `medium`, `low`), `ad_recommendation`, `window_days`, `snapshot_date`.
  - Уникальный составной индекс `ix_audience_metrics_platform_cat_date` (`platform`, `category_slug`, `snapshot_date`) для быстрого извлечения временных рядов.
  - Успешное применение миграции `0015` на боевом сервере PostgreSQL на проде.

### Сервис аналитики аудитории и таргетинга (`app/services/audience_analytics.py`)
- **Интеграция с Яндекс.Метрикой (Сайт и Дзен)**:
  - Прямой опрос Reporting API по срезу путей первого уровня (`ym:s:startURLPath`) с метриками `ym:s:visits,ym:s:users,ym:s:bounceRate,ym:s:avgVisitDurationSeconds` (свыше 2 100 путей сайта `svodka.press`).
  - Опрос счётчика Дзена (`metrika_dzen_counter_id = 106439611`): подтверждена активность счётчика и сбор визитов/пользователей.
- **Интеллектуальный маппинг 20 рубрик**:
  - Функция `_extract_category_slug_from_path` парсит URL-структуру `svodka.press/<category_slug>/<post_slug>/`, сопоставляя реальный трафик с 20 редакционными рубриками и вычисляя долю аудитории.
- **Алгоритм расчета Ad Potential Index (0.0 — 100.0 баллов)**:
  - `Volume Score (0-35)`: относительный объём аудитории рубрики.
  - `Engagement Score (0-35)`: глубина чтения (оптимум 120+ сек) и удержание (минимизация отказов).
  - `Commercial Intent (0-30)`: рыночный вес тематики по ставкам eCPM (IT, AI, финансы, недвижимость, медицина).
  - Присвоение уровней готовности: `high` (60+), `medium` (40-60), `low` (<40).
- **Синтез рекомендаций ИИ для будущей страницы `/ads`**:
  - Подбор формата рекламных интеграций под специфику аудитории каждой рубрики (B2B-лиды, e-commerce, нативные врезки, прилипающие баннеры).
  - Автоматическое сохранение снимков в БД с безопасной обработкой транзакций (`hasattr(session, "rollback")` и `commit`).

### Интерфейс дашборда на `/seo` (`app/api/dashboard.py`, `app/templates/dashboard/seo.html`)
- **Вкладка «👥 Читатели: Сайт vs Дзен & /ads»**:
  - Bento-сравнение каналов: сайт (визиты, пользователи, среднее время, отказы) vs канал Дзен.
  - Карточки лидеров конверсионного потенциала с персональными рекомендациями ИИ.
  - Интерактивная матрица всех 20 редакционных рубрик с бейджами готовности (`Готово к монетизации`, `Тестирование`, `Низкий потенциал`) и прогресс-барами долей аудитории.
  - Прозрачный информационный блок о подготовке отдельного интерфейса управления рекламой `/ads`.

### Верификация и стабильность
- `ruff check app/ tests/`: 0 ошибок.
- 5 тестов `tests/test_audience_analytics.py`, 103 теста `tests/test_dashboard_ui.py`, 61 тест `tests/test_seo_analytics.py`: 100% pass (всего 169+ тестов).
- Деплой на боевой VPS `144.31.141.17`, сервисы перезапущены, прямой live-запрос к `/seo` подтвердил HTTP 200 OK (80 449 байт).

## [2.24.0] — 2026-09-27 — Индексация архива 8 700+ статей WordPress, Rank Math updateMeta, Anti-Boring генерация и аудит ИИ-провайдеров

### Индексация архива WordPress и внутренняя перелинковка (`app/services/wp_archive.py`, `app/services/lab_pipeline.py`, `app/services/research.py`)
- **Сервис `WPArchiveService`**:
  - Подключение к REST API WordPress (`GET /wp/v2/posts?per_page=100&_fields=id,title,slug,link,date`), извлечение общего числа постов по заголовку `x-wp-total` (8 701 статья на `svodka.press`).
  - Многоуровневое кэширование: Redis (`wp:archive:posts`, TTL 1 час) с автоматическим fallback в оперативную память процесса.
  - Семантический поиск релевантных статей по ключевым сущностям (`find_related`), автоматическая вставка контекстных ссылок и блоков «Читайте по теме».
- **Статус в Лаборатории**:
  - Карточка статуса (`lab_status_card.html`) отображает реальное число `8 701 статья на сайте`.
  - В карточке генерации (`lab_generate_result.html`) бейдж «Архив пуст» заменён на «Тема новая для сайта» (если прямых пересечений нет) с указанием активного архива из 8 700+ постов.

### Прямая запись метаполей Rank Math (`app/services/wp_publisher.py`)
- **Эндпоинт `/wp-json/rankmath/v1/updateMeta`**:
  - Реализован метод `_update_rank_math_meta(post_id, meta)`: прямая отправка `objectType: "post"`, `objectID`, `meta` (`rank_math_title`, `rank_math_description`, `rank_math_focus_keyword`, `rank_math_robots`).
  - Гарантированная запись SEO-сниппетов в базу данных WordPress и вывод в `<head>`, в обход ограничений `show_in_rest` стандартного REST API.
  - Подавление предупреждений о сбросе метаданных при подтверждении сохранения плагином Rank Math.

### Качество генерации и глубокий контекст ресерча (`app/services/content_prompts.py`, `app/services/content_generator.py`, `app/services/lab_pipeline.py`)
- **Расширенный контекст**: лимит текста внешнего ресерча увеличен с 400 до 1500 символов на источник под блоком `=== КОНТЕКСТ И АНАЛИТИЧЕСКИЕ ДАННЫЕ ИЗ ВНЕШНЕГО РЕСЕРЧА ===`.
- **Anti-boring директива**: запрет сухих отписок и пересказов телеграм-постов; строгое требование внедрять цифры, предысторию и аналитику из веб-ресерча.
- **Инъекция стиля**: выбранный редактором стиль и кастомные инструкции (`prompt_voice`) пробрасываются в `generate_article` и внедряются в системный/пользовательский промт.
- **Защита публикации**: исправление ошибки `alt_text` при загрузке медиа в WordPress; автоматический выбор первой качественной иллюстрации из внешнего веб-ресерча (`web_images`), если оператор не выбрал обложку вручную.

### Аудит и проверка ИИ-провайдеров (`app/services/gemini_key_pool.py`, `app/services/ai_provider_keys.py`)
- **Диагностика кастомного шлюза `@iishkogatewaybot` (`https://grok.reforrm.me/v1`)**:
  - Выявлен ответ HTTP 401 `Invalid token (new_api_error)` со стороны сервера New API (`grok.reforrm.me`). Ключ отклонён удалённым шлюзом как недействительный/неактивированный.
- **Массовая верификация пула Google AI Studio**:
  - Протестированы все 50 ключей Google в базе: 42 ключа полностью исправны и активны, включая все 10 вновь добавленных ключей (ID 46–55).

### Устранение сбоя 500 на странице `/seo` (`app/api/dashboard.py`, миграция `0014_seo_optimizations`)
- **Применение миграции схемы на VPS**: файл миграции `0014_seo_optimizations` доставлен в контейнеры, выполнена команда `alembic upgrade head`, таблица `seo_optimizations` успешно создана в базе данных PostgreSQL на проде.
- **Транзакционная защита и откат**: в маршруте `dashboard_seo` в блоках обработки исключений внедрён обязательный вызов `await session.rollback()`. Это предотвращает сбой `InFailedSQLTransactionError` при любых ошибках отдельных SQL-запросов.
- **Отказоустойчивая загрузка истории и паттернов**: загрузка истории оптимизаций и выученных паттернов изолирована в независимые try-except блоки с дефолтными значениями, что исключает падение всей страницы `/seo`.

## [2.23.0] — 2026-09-27 — Самообучаемая SEO-оптимизация (Rank Math 1-Click + IndexNow), устранение артефактов альбомов Telegram и лимит очереди Lab

### Самообучаемая оптимизация сниппетов (`app/models/seo_optimization.py`, `app/services/seo_learning.py`, `app/services/wp_publisher.py`, `app/api/dashboard.py`)
- **Модель базы знаний `SeoOptimization`**:
  - Сохранение срезов эффективности (клики, показы, CTR, средняя позиция) до и после оптимизации.
  - Сохранение предложений ИИ: гипотеза, SEO Title, SEO Description, Focus Keyword, обоснование и статус жизненного цикла (`draft` -> `applied` -> `evaluating` -> `winner`/`neutral`/`loser`).
  - Миграция схемы Alembic `0014_seo_optimizations` применена на продакшене.
- **Обучение на победах**:
  - `generate_seo_improvement`: автоматически извлекает из БД успешные оптимизации (`is_winner = true`) и передаёт их модели в качестве few-shot примеров удачных приёмов повышения кликабельности в SERP.
- **1-Click обновление WordPress Rank Math**:
  - `WPPublisherService.update_post_seo_snippet`: обновляет метаполя `rank_math_title`, `rank_math_description`, `rank_math_focus_keyword` через WordPress REST API.
  - Моментальный пинг протокола IndexNow при успешном применении сниппета для внеочередного переобхода поисковыми ботами Bing, Yandex и др.
- **Интерфейс на `/seo`**:
  - Модальное окно оптимизации с живым предпросмотром сниппета Google/Yandex, редактором формулировок и кнопкой «⚡ Применить в WordPress и уведомить поисковики».
  - Таблица истории принятых решений с фиксацией дельты CTR и статуса выученной эффективности.

### Устранение расщепления альбомов Telegram и пустых постов (`app/workers/ingestion_worker.py`, `app/services/deduplicator.py`)
- **Водяной знак альбомов `telegram_watermark`**:
  - Учтены не только `external_post_id`, но и все `original_post_id` из `media_data`. Это исключает повторную вычитку хвостовых сообщений альбомов без подписи.
  - В `_insert_candidate` добавлена проверка наличия медиа для отсечения пустых постов-осколков.
- **Фильтрация постов без текста в дедупликаторе**:
  - Посты без текста (`raw_text.strip() == ""`) маркируются `DedupStatus.SKIPPED_DUPLICATE` с причиной `empty_text` и `cluster_id = None`. Они больше не создают пустые кластеры `raw_news #...`.
  - Удалены 14 накопившихся пустых кластеров из PostgreSQL.

### Управление очередью сюжетов в Лаборатории (`app/services/lab_pipeline.py`, `app/api/dashboard.py`, `app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_groups_list.html`)
- **Честный подсчёт очереди**:
  - `get_news_groups_summary` возвращает реальное количество сюжетов в БД (`total_clusters`), количество отображаемых (`displayed_clusters`), и поддерживает пагинацию/лимит.
- **HTMX-селектор лимита**:
  - В шапке списка добавлен интерактивный переключатель `50 | 100 | 250 | Все ({total})` и маршрут `GET /lab/groups` для быстрой подгрузки без перезагрузки всей страницы.

## [2.22.1] — 2026-09-27 — Google Search Console: Domain Property Auto-Resolution & ISO Date Formats

### Search Console Property Auto-Discovery & Normalization (`app/services/google_connection.py`, `app/api/dashboard.py`)
- **Domain Property Matching (`resolve_gsc_site_url`)**:
  - Automatically queries `GET https://www.googleapis.com/webmasters/v3/sites` to inspect verified properties accessible to the Service Account.
  - Matches bare domain inputs (e.g. `svodka.press`) or URL prefixes (`https://svodka.press/`) to Search Console Domain Properties (`sc-domain:svodka.press`) and vice versa.
  - Auto-selects single available domain property if no input was specified.
  - Persists resolved property identifier (`sc-domain:svodka.press`) to PostgreSQL on test probe or save.
- **Search Analytics ISO Date Strictness Fix**:
  - Fixed HTTP 400 error in `searchAnalytics/query`: replaced relative `"7daysAgo"` with strict ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` date strings calculated via `datetime.now(UTC).date()`.
  - Accurately captures 7-day impressions and clicks without API rejection.
- **Help & UI Guidance (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/google_services_card.html`)**:
  - Updated guide and input placeholders explaining `sc-domain:` properties vs URL prefixes.

### Verification
- `ruff check`: 0 errors across all modified modules and tests.

- UI tests: `tests/test_dashboard_ui.py` (103 passed in 53.12s).

## [2.22.0] — 2026-09-27 — Multi-Search Analytics & Webmasters: Google Analytics 4, Search Console, Bing Webmaster & IndexNow Protocol

### Google Services Integration (GA4, Search Console, MCP)
- **Asynchronous RS256 JWT Service Account Minting (`app/services/google_connection.py`)**:
  - Implemented lightweight, fully async token minting using pure `pyjwt` and `cryptography` without bulky Google Cloud SDKs (`google-auth`, `google-api-python-client`), reducing container footprint.
  - Generates signed OAuth2 assertions with standard Google scopes (`analytics.readonly`, `webmasters.readonly`) against `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token`.
  - In-memory token caching with thread-safe `asyncio.Lock` and 55-minute TTL buffer.
- **Diagnostic Probes**:
  - `probe_google_analytics`: tests live connection via GA4 Data API v1beta (`:runReport`), fetching 7-day traffic stats (`activeUsers`, `sessions`, `screenPageViews`).
  - `probe_google_search_console`: verifies site ownership and queries 7-day search visibility metrics (`clicks`, `impressions`, `rowLimit=5`) via Google Webmasters Search Analytics API.
- **Fernet Encryption & Masking**:
  - Securely encrypts `service_account_json` and `oauth_access_token` at rest in PostgreSQL (`system_settings`, key `google_services_connection`).
  - Renders masked secrets and extracts `client_email` for operator convenience.
- **Bento Card & Interactive Setup Guide (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/google_services_card.html`)**:
  - Three-column setup guide explaining GCP Console setup, API activation, GA4 Viewer role assignment, and Search Console Full/Restricted user delegation.
  - Dedicated form and local HTMX test buttons with immediate feedback.

### Bing Webmaster Tools & IndexNow Instant Indexing
- **Bing Webmaster API Client (`app/services/bing_connection.py`)**:
  - Encrypted storage for 32-character Bing API key.
  - `probe_bing_webmaster`: validates key and retrieves verified sites via `GetUserSites?apikey=...`.
- **IndexNow Protocol Engine (`app/services/bing_connection.py`)**:
  - Submits newly published URLs to `https://api.indexnow.org/IndexNow` (notifying Bing, Yandex, Seznam, and Naver simultaneously).
  - Handles HTTP 200 (OK) and 202 (Accepted) response codes, custom key locations, and host normalisation.
- **Automatic Publishing Hook (`app/services/lab_pipeline.py`, `app/workers/publisher_worker.py`)**:
  - Non-blocking automatic trigger: upon article publication (both scheduled runs via `publisher_worker` and manual Lab clicks in `run_cluster_publish_step`), the newly created WordPress permalink is submitted to IndexNow immediately.
- **Bento Card & Setup Guide (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/bing_services_card.html`)**:
  - Guide on importing sites from Google Search Console in 1 click and deploying the IndexNow key verification file.

### Dashboard Settings & Generic Diagnostic UI
- **Settings Dashboard Integration (`app/templates/dashboard/settings.html`, `app/api/dashboard.py`)**:
  - Integrated both Google and Bing Bento cards into `/settings` alongside WordPress and Yandex cards.
  - Endpoints: `POST /settings/google/save`, `POST /settings/google/test-ga4`, `POST /settings/google/test-gsc`, `POST /settings/bing/save`, `POST /settings/bing/test-bing`, `POST /settings/bing/test-indexnow`.
- **Reusable Diagnostic Partial (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/service_test_result.html`)**:
  - Generic HIG result container with color-coded success/error pills, detail view, and dismiss button.

### Documentation & Tooling
- Created `docs/12_GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_MCP.md` detailing GA4 Data API, scopes, and MCP sidecar configuration (`@googleanalytics/google-analytics-mcp`).
- Updated `docs/09_SEO_ANALYTICS.md` documenting implemented Google and Bing/IndexNow features.

### Verification
- `ruff check`: 0 errors across all modified modules and tests.
- Unit tests: `tests/test_google_connection.py` (4 tests), `tests/test_bing_connection.py` (4 tests), `tests/test_yandex_connection.py` (3 tests) pass (11 passed in 0.75s).
- Full Dashboard UI test suite: 103 passed in 52.85s.

### Web Research Relevance & Candidate Filtering
- **Russian & English Inflection Stemmer (`app/services/web_researcher.py`)**:
  - Implemented lightweight `_stem(word)` to strip common Russian noun/adjective grammatical endings (`-ов`, `-ев`, `-ами`, `-ями`, `-ого`, `-его`, `-ский`, `-ской`, `-ая`, `-ое`, `-ые`, `-ий`, etc.) and English suffixes (`-ing`, `-ed`, `-es`, `-s`).
  - Solves lexical overlap mismatch where inflected forms (`модель` vs `модели`, `китайская` vs `китайский`) yielded zero similarity without full NLP dependencies.
- **Entity & Topical Term Extraction (`app/services/web_researcher.py`)**:
  - Extracted proper nouns, brand/model names with internal punctuation (`GLM-5.3`, `Z.ai`), capitalized terms, numbers, and key non-stopword tokens.
- **Candidate Over-Sampling & Semantic Relevance Gating (`app/services/web_researcher.py`)**:
  - Over-sampled search results: queries up to `max_sources * 2` (up to 6) candidates before filtering.
  - Implemented `evaluate_article_relevance(article, cluster_title, cluster_summary)`: computes composite score (50% core entity match, 30% stemmed keyword overlap, 20% title lexical match).
  - Gating threshold: candidates scoring below 0.30 or having 0 core entity matches are rejected as off-topic (e.g. discarding car articles when researching an AI model).
  - Studio Step 2 UI displays relevance verification badges (`✓ В тему (XX%)` or `⚠️ Контекст (XX%)`).

### Media Classification & Gallery Controls
- **Image Type Classifier (`app/services/web_researcher.py`)**:
  - Added `classify_web_image(url, alt, context_text, width, height)` categorizing web images into:
    - `photo` (Репортажное фото)
    - `infographic` (Инфографика / Таблица / Бенчмарк)
    - `logo` (Логотип / Иконка)
    - `graphic` (Баннер / Графика)
  - `fetch_clean_article` discards logos, icons, and diagrams, prioritizing authentic documentary photos.
- **Interactive Studio Gallery Controls (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_media_result.html`, `lab_cluster_detail.html`, `lab.html`)**:
  - Media badges render image kind (`📸 Фото`, `📊 Таблица/График`, `🏷 Логотип`) alongside the publisher domain.
  - Added «⭐ На обложку» button: highlights the selected image with an emerald border, sets `#lab-selected-cover-url`, and persists it to `cluster.enrichment_data["selected_cover_url"]`.
  - Added «✕ Скрыть» button: instantly hides irrelevant, misclassified, or unwanted images from the view.
  - Automatic WordPress Media Upload: during publication step (`run_cluster_publish_step`), if `selected_cover_url` is present, uploads it via `WPMediaService.upload_image()` and attaches it as `featured_media`.

### Deep Prompt Visibility & Textarea
- **Editable Prompt Textarea (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_cluster_detail.html`, `lab.html`)**:
  - Expanded Step 3 prompt textarea to `rows="7"` with monospace typography (`font-mono text-xs`).
  - Embedded `data-prompts` mapping all 5 deep editorial prompts directly on the container.
  - Dynamic switching hook (`switchLabStyle`): instantly populates the textarea when changing the style dropdown without stub placeholders.

### Yandex Services Diagnostics & Interactive Guide
- **Inline Diagnostic Results (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/yandex_services_card.html`)**:
  - Moved diagnostic results from the bottom of the card directly into local containers under each service block (`#wordstat-test-result`, `#metrika-test-result`, `#webmaster-test-result`).
  - Added button loading state (`hx-disabled-elt="this"`) so test feedback is immediate and in-view without scrolling.
- **Wordstat Search API Schema Validation (`app/services/yandex_connection.py`)**:
  - Fixed parameter in `probe_yandex_wordstat` payload from plural `phrases: [...]` to singular `phrase: "новости"`, matching the Yandex Cloud Search API v2 contract.
- **Interactive Bento Setup Guide (`app/templates/dashboard/partials/yandex_services_card.html`)**:
  - Added collapsible guide button «📖 Справка: как подключить» in card header.
  - Three-column guide detailing exact step-by-step instructions, links, and scope names for Yandex Metrika (`metrika:read`, `metrika:write`), Webmaster (`webmaster:verify`, `webmaster:hostinfo`), and Wordstat (`yc.search-api.execute` vs AI emulation).

### Verification

- Unit tests: `tests/test_web_research_relevance.py` (4 tests) and `tests/test_lab_stepwise.py` (7 tests) pass (11 passed in 3.36s).
- Live verification: Yandex Metrika and Webmaster APIs tested and confirmed with HTTP 200 OK.

## [2.20.0] — 2026-09-27 — Web Research Token Preservation, Web Media Proxy, Dynamic Style Prompts & Yandex Integration Card

### Web Research & External Media
- **Search Query Token Preservation (`app/services/web_researcher.py`)**:
  - Replaced naive period splitting (`raw.split('.')[0]`) with sentence boundary regex `(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[А-ЯA-Z0-9«\"])|\n+`.
  - Preserved internal dots and version numbers in tokens (e.g. `Z.ai`, `GLM-5.3`, `domain.ru`).
  - Fixed false-positive query generation where Chinese AI story about `Z.ai` was incorrectly truncated to `Китайская Z` and pulled car news.
  - Retained natural grammatical prepositions in search query tokens (up to 8 words) for authentic DuckDuckGo/Yandex news queries.

## [2.18.8] — 2026-09-26 — Lab News Ingestion Restoration & Numeric Telegram Peer Resolution

### Telegram MTProto Ingestion & Key Realignment
- **Root Cause Diagnosis**:
  - Ingestion runs in the Lab triggered `MTProtoIngestor`, which attempted to claim and decrypt the primary Telegram account credentials (+79055342403) from `telegram_accounts`.
  - In `/opt/svodkapress/.env`, `FERNET_KEY` was set to an unmatching key (`HsGi310n...`), raising `InvalidToken` during `decrypt_session()`.
  - Because of 3 consecutive failed decryption attempts, the pool marked the Telegram account as broken (`failure_count = 3`, `is_active = False`).
  - With no active MTProto accounts available, ingestion fell back to the legacy GramJS bridge. Since `GRAMJS_TOKEN` was intentionally omitted on production (Telethon is the primary ingestor), all 36 active sources were skipped with 0 posts collected.
  - Additionally, all 45 encrypted AI provider keys stored in PostgreSQL were undecryptable under the mismatched key.
- **Key Recovery & Account Reactivation**:
  - Located the authoritative `FERNET_KEY` (`WRx3Ep2KqrdIBsNB7BjY0DbWLdiRCjcQAnJgYz8nztc=`) from verified production backup.
  - Restored `FERNET_KEY` in `/opt/svodkapress/.env`. Verified that 100% of stored secrets (45 AI provider keys and the Telegram session string) decrypt cleanly.
  - Reset account state in `telegram_accounts`: `UPDATE telegram_accounts SET is_active = true, failure_count = 0, last_error = NULL WHERE id = 1;`. Verified Telethon authorization as `@Genadich2403`.
- **Numeric Telegram Channel Resolution (`app/services/telegram_mtproto.py`)**:
  - Added `_coerce_channel_target(channel: str) -> int | str` to parse numeric channel IDs (e.g. `-1001117628569` for Mash, `-1001214468686` for svodka38) into integers before passing them to Telethon.
  - In `_resolve_peer()`, added automatic on-demand dialog cache warming (`await self._client.get_dialogs()`) on `ValueError` so private and numeric channels resolve even on newly connected in-memory `StringSession` instances.
  - Updated `mark_read()` with integer target coercion.
- **Verification**:
  - Unit tests: 99/99 passed in `tests/test_telegram_accounts.py`.
  - Type checking (`mypy`) & linting (`ruff`): 0 errors.
  - Live production ingestion pass: 1,345 posts fetched, 1,027 posts inserted into `raw_news`, 976 news clusters created.
  - Lab UI: `get_news_groups_summary` returns 50 rich news groups ready for editorial processing.

## [2.18.7] — 2026-09-26 — Production Reverse Proxy Alignment (svodkanews.team-big.ru / NPM)

### Infrastructure & Reverse Proxy Fix
- **Root Cause Diagnosis**:
  - `svodkanews.team-big.ru` resolves to edge proxy `150.241.98.134` running Nginx Proxy Manager (OpenResty).
  - NPM proxy host 4 was forwarding traffic to `http://144.31.141.17:8000`.
  - On VPS `144.31.141.17`, iptables `DOCKER-USER` and UFW were configured to allow incoming connections on port 8000 strictly from `150.241.98.134` (and drop all other public traffic).
  - However, in `/opt/svodkapress/.env` on the VPS:
    - `APP_HOST_PORT` was set to `58000` (port mismatch).
    - `APP_BIND_ADDRESS` was omitted (causing Docker to bind solely to `127.0.0.1`, rejecting external packets from the reverse proxy).
    - `ALLOWED_HOSTS` only contained `127.0.0.1,localhost` (omitting `svodkanews.team-big.ru`).
    - `TRUSTED_PROXY_HOSTS` only contained `127.0.0.1` (omitting NPM proxy IPs `150.241.98.134,150.241.98.133`).
- **Configuration & Deployment Update**:
  - Updated `/opt/svodkapress/.env` on `144.31.141.17`:
    - `APP_HOST_PORT=8000`
    - `APP_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0`
    - `ALLOWED_HOSTS=svodkanews.team-big.ru,127.0.0.1,localhost,144.31.141.17`
    - `TRUSTED_PROXY_HOSTS=127.0.0.1,150.241.98.134,150.241.98.133`
    - `APP_ENV=production` & `DEBUG=false`
    - Secured file permissions to `600`.
  - Executed `./scripts/deploy_vps.sh --skip-build` to recreate the application stack.
- **Verification**:
  - All 7 containers are healthy and UP on `144.31.141.17`.
  - Liveness and readiness endpoints returned 200 OK.
  - Public endpoint verified: `curl -sSL -D - https://svodkanews.team-big.ru/ -o /dev/null` returns `HTTP/2 303` (redirecting to `/login?next=/`) followed by `HTTP/2 200` with the complete HTML dashboard interface.

### Intelligent Error-Aware Classification (`classify_model_error`)
- `app/services/model_pool.py`:
  - Added `ErrorCategory` enum (`TRANSIENT`, `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`, `FORMAT_REJECTION`, `AUTH_CREDENTIALS`, `UNKNOWN`) and `ErrorClassification` dataclass.
  - Implemented `classify_model_error(error)` and `_classify_status_code(status, error)`:
    - Transient errors (HTTP 429, 500, 502, 503, 504, network timeouts): assigns 60s cooldown and transitions to next fallback model.
    - Fatal model errors (HTTP 404 Not Found, 402 Insufficient Balance/Payment Required): assigns 3600s cooldown, marks model `is_working = False` in PostgreSQL via `deactivate_broken_model`, and transitions to fallback.
    - Auth errors (HTTP 401, 403): assigns 3600s cooldown with fallback.
    - Format errors (`json.JSONDecodeError`, Pydantic schema validation failures): assigns 120s cooldown with fallback.
    - Unknown system errors (e.g. `AssertionError`): fails immediately without cycling through fallback models.
  - Implemented `_safe_fire_task(coro)`: wraps background coroutines with error logging to eliminate unhandled task exceptions and test runner warnings.

### Cross-Provider Fallback Chains (`_build_chain_for_role`)
- `app/services/gemini_key_pool.py`:
  - Implemented `_build_chain_for_role(primary_id, candidates, chat_options, max_chain=3)`: enforces provider diversity by guaranteeing that the first fallback candidate is selected from a different provider than the primary model (e.g., custom gateway `api.dollariuy.fun` -> Google AI Studio -> vsllm.cc).
  - Updated `auto_select_best_models(session)`: computes resilient fallback chains for all roles, persists them to `system_settings` (`ai_model_chains`), and returns primary role mappings.

### Self-Healing Runtime Routing (`auto_heal_role_routing`)
- `app/services/model_pool.py`:
  - Implemented `auto_heal_role_routing(role, failed_model, successful_model, reason, session=None)`: dynamically promotes a successful fallback model to primary in `system_settings` (`ai_model_routing`) when the primary model fails. Logs operator warning so subsequent calls route directly to the healthy model.
  - Implemented `_try_emergency_fallbacks`: dynamically inspects `AIProviderModel` for any verified working model if all models in a role chain are exhausted.
  - Updated `run_with_fallback`: fully orchestrates classification, cooldowns, deactivation, auto-healing, and emergency fallback.

### Pipeline Integration & UI Display
- `app/services/lab_pipeline.py`:
  - Updated `_ai_classify_rubric` / `classify_cluster_category` to resolve `arbiter` chain and execute via `run_with_fallback`.

  - Formatted auto-select alert banner to display visual chains (`Арбитр [grok-4.5 ➔ gemini-3.1-flash-lite ➔ gemini-3.5-flash-lite]`).

- `app/services/gemini_key_pool.py`:

### Dynamic Gemini `ListModels` Discovery & Intelligent Fallback
- `app/services/gemini_key_pool.py`:
  - Implemented `query_gemini_models()`: calls Google AI Studio `ModelService.ListModels` (`GET /v1beta/models` with `x-goog-api-key`) to poll available models for the given key and enumerate `supportedGenerationMethods`.
  - Dynamically discovers text/vision models (`generateContent`) and embedding models (`embedContent`) per key.
  - Graceful fallback for missing embedding models: if a key lacks an `embedContent` model or returns HTTP 404, embeddings capability is marked inactive (gray badge) while key health remains 🟢 HEALTHY, seamlessly falling back to local `fastembed` for deduplication.
  - Clear human-readable error messages for HTTP 401/403 (invalid key / project restrictions) and HTTP 429 (rate limits).

### False-Positive Filter Recovery («Отфильтровано»)
- `app/services/text_utils.py`:
  - Added `FILTER_REASON_LABELS` and `describe_filter_reason()` to provide human-readable Russian descriptions for filtering triggers (`digest_structure`, `commercial_promo`, `ad_markers`, `empty_text`, `language_mismatch`).
  - Adjusted `_BULLET_ITEM_RE` to ignore `⚡️` emojis at the start of breaking news items, preventing false-positive digest flags on hot news.
  - Added major news acronyms (`SHOT`, `MASH`, `TASS`, `NEWS`, `RT`, `RIA`) to `_CAPS_CODE_STOPWORDS` to prevent false promo code flags.
- `app/services/lab_pipeline.py`:
  - Implemented `unblock_raw_post()`: resets `is_digest`, `is_ad`, clears filter reasons, restores status to `DedupStatus.UNIQUE`, recalculates MinHash signature, and binds or creates a new `NewsCluster`.

## [2.16.0] — 2026-09-24 — Stepwise Pipeline Laboratory (Apple macOS HIG Redesign)

### Stepwise Laboratory Service
- `app/services/lab_pipeline.py`:
  - `get_lab_system_status()`: honest system metrics calculation (total non-duplicate sources, active sources, sources with media, uncollected fresh sources, queued posts/clusters, published articles, and automatic first-pass detection).
  - `get_news_groups_summary()`: groups ingested raw news into clusters, calculates source coverage breadth, identifies primary post (similarity 1.0) and all associated duplicates (via direct cluster link and `dedup_matched_cluster_id` entity link), tracks MinHash L1 / Semantic L2 levels, separates heuristic-filtered posts (digests, ads).
  - `get_cluster_detail()`: full master-detail inspection context for one cluster (all primary and duplicate posts with URLs and snippets, token-free facts, archive article cross-references, media verdicts, and article draft).
  - `run_cluster_research_step()`: zero-token fact collection (`collect_facts`) and archive interlinking (`find_related_articles`) saved into `cluster.enrichment_data`.
  - `run_cluster_media_step()`: OCR watermark inspection for photos in the cluster.
  - `run_cluster_generate_step()`: AI moderation (`moderate_cluster`), editorial voice selection across 5 angles, content generation (`generate_article`), and anti-slop validation (`analyze_slop`, `clean_ai_slop`).
  - `run_cluster_publish_step()`: WordPress REST publishing via `get_active_wp_publisher()`, updates article to `WP_PUBLISHED` with live link.

### Apple macOS HIG Web UI & HTMX Split Studio
- `app/templates/dashboard/lab.html`: Complete redesign using Apple Light macOS HIG aesthetic (`#FFFFFF` cards, `#F5F5F7` backdrop, hairline borders `border-black/[0.08]`, and SF Pro/Inter typography).
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_status_card.html`: Bento grid status card showing sources (active vs total), post queue, clusters, first pass indicator, and quick control buttons («Сбросить очередь и собрать (по 50)», «Собрать без сброса», «Очистить очередь»).
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_groups_list.html`: Step 1 News Groups view with source coverage badges (e.g. «25 источников»), filter pills (Все / С дубликатами / Одиночные / Отфильтрованные), channel pills, and expandable drawer displaying the primary post and every duplicate with similarity score and direct Telegram link.
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_cluster_detail.html`: Step 2 Stepwise Studio for selected cluster with 4 sequential stepper cards:
  - Шаг 1: Факты и архивные связи (0 токенов).
  - Шаг 2: Медиа и OCR водяных знаков.
  - Шаг 3: AI Генерация и Анти-слоп с выбором одного из 5 стилистических углов и вводом инструкций.
  - Шаг 4: Публикация в WordPress с получением ссылки на живой сайт.
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_main_container.html`: Master Split-Studio layout linking left master list with right stepwise studio.
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/lab_research_result.html`, `lab_media_result.html`, `lab_generate_result.html`, `lab_publish_result.html`: HTMX fragment templates for step-by-step UI responses.

### Dashboard API Endpoints
- `app/api/dashboard.py`:
  - `GET /lab`: renders redesigned laboratory with honest system metrics and news groups.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/collect-reset`: clears queue and triggers first-pass collection (50 posts per channel) and MinHash deduplication.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/collect-only`: runs incremental collection without resetting queue.
  - `GET /api/ui/lab/cluster/{cluster_id}`: loads cluster details into the Stepwise Studio panel.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/cluster/{cluster_id}/research`: executes research step for cluster.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/cluster/{cluster_id}/media`: executes OCR watermark check for cluster.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/cluster/{cluster_id}/generate`: executes AI generation and anti-slop cleaning.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/cluster/{cluster_id}/publish`: executes WordPress publication.
  - `POST /api/ui/lab/reset`: updated to support `#lab-main-container` swap with full backward compatibility.

## [2.15.0] — 2026-09-24 — WordPress SSH & REST Dynamic Connection, Fernet Encryption & Category Sync

### WordPress Connection Service & Secret Storage
- `app/services/wp_connection.py`:
  - `WPConnectionConfig` data structure handling REST API (`wp_base_url`, `wp_app_user`, `wp_app_password`) and SSH credentials (`ssh_host`, `ssh_user`, `ssh_port`, `ssh_key_path`, `ssh_private_key`, `wp_document_root`).
  - Fernet encryption via `SecretBox`: credentials stored securely in `system_settings` (`wp_connection`); secrets are never stored in plaintext and masked on public UI rendering.
  - `load_wp_connection()` & `save_wp_connection()`: database configuration takes precedence over `.env` fallbacks.
  - `test_wp_rest()`: probes `/wp-json/wp/v2/users/me` to verify user capabilities (`publish_posts`) and authentication validity.
  - `test_wp_ssh()`: asyncssh probe verifying SSH connection, host key acceptance, WordPress root directory, `wp-config.php`/`wp-load.php`, and core version.
  - `sync_wp_categories()`: fetches `/wp-json/wp/v2/categories?per_page=100`, upserts categories into `CategoryPrompt`, and updates runtime `CATEGORY_MAP`.
  - `get_active_wp_publisher()`: factory returning `WPPublisher` initialized with active database credentials.

### Web UI Dashboard & HTMX Diagnostics
- `app/templates/dashboard/settings.html`: Integrated WordPress Bento connection card.
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/wp_connection_card.html`: Apple Light HIG Bento card with connection status badge, Fernet encryption indicator, masked password inputs, and live diagnostic buttons.
- `app/templates/dashboard/partials/wp_test_result.html` & `wp_sync_result.html`: HTMX response fragments rendering real-time REST/SSH probe diagnostics and synced category chips.
- `app/api/dashboard.py`:
  - Added `wp_conn` to `dashboard_settings` template context.
  - Registered `POST /api/ui/settings/wordpress/save`: saves encrypted parameters and re-renders connection card.
  - Registered `POST /api/ui/settings/wordpress/test`: triggers live REST & SSH probes with formatted diagnostic card.
  - Registered `POST /api/ui/settings/wordpress/sync_categories`: triggers taxonomy synchronization and renders category chips.

### Verification & Documentation
- `tests/test_wp_connection.py`: 6 tests passing (100% pass) verifying load/save, Fernet encryption, REST diagnostic mocks, SSH validation, and category synchronization.
- `tests/test_dashboard_ui.py`: 98 tests passing (100% pass) including 5 new integration tests for WordPress connection card, save, test, and sync endpoints.
- `docs/07_DISTRIBUTION_WP.md`: Updated to document SSH and dynamic taxonomy features as implemented.
- `docs/10_WEB_UI_DASHBOARD.md`: Documented `/settings` WordPress & SSH connection card.

## [2.11.0] — 2026-09-04 — Phase 1 (part): sources page, dialog import, freshness gate

**Deployed to production 2026-09-04.** Host `144.31.141.17`, `/opt/svodkapress`,
copy deployment. Backups first: database dump
`/opt/backups/svodkapress-20260904-104531-pre-0006.sql.gz` (gzip verified) and
code archive `/opt/backups/code-20260904-104531-pre-2.11.0.tar.gz`. Migration
`0005_article_journals → 0006_source_dialog_fields` applied; `/health` and
`/health/ready` pass; all three ARQ workers running; ports 8000 and 3005 remain
filtered in `DOCKER-USER`. The pipeline switch is still off, so no AI spend.

### Telegram accounts are connected from the panel

- `app/services/telegram_auth.py` — added `TelegramQrLogin` on Telethon's own
  `client.qr_login()`, plus `TelegramAuthFlow.resume()` / `export_session()` so
  the phone flow survives gunicorn's two workers.
- `app/services/telegram_login.py` (new) — Fernet-encrypted, TTL-bounded sign-in
  state in Redis. QR waits in the worker that exported the token, because
  Telethon delivers `UpdateLoginToken` on that live client only.
- `app/services/telegram_accounts.py` — `disconnect()` clears the stored session
  and deactivates the account; `credentials_for()` decrypts one loaded row so
  the importer can walk every account without stamping the LRU cursor.
- Dashboard endpoints: QR start, phone start, code, 2FA password, poll, and
  disconnect (Telegram `log_out()` is best-effort; the local session is always
  cleared). `scripts/auth_telegram.py` remains as the fallback path.

### Every dialog is listed, with a link and a description

- `app/services/telegram_mtproto.py` — `list_dialogs()` returns channels,
  supergroups, gigagroups, groups, bots and private chats; `classify_peer`,
  `peer_identifier`, `peer_invite_link`, `kind_label`. About-text comes from
  `GetFull*` and stops after the first flood wait rather than parking the account.
- Ingestion no longer rejects non-broadcast peers: the operator's checkbox is
  the filter. `_resolve_peer` replaced `_resolve_channel`.
- Import unions dialogs across all healthy accounts, matching existing rows by
  identifier or `telegram_id`; new rows arrive inactive.
- Migration `0006_source_dialog_fields` adds `sources.peer_kind`, `username`,
  `description`, `invite_link`.

### Freshness: never collect older than today

- `app/services/ingest_freshness.py` (new) — cutoff at 00:00 `Europe/Moscow`.
- Applied on a first pass and on a resume whose last scrape was a previous
  Moscow day, in both the Telegram reader and the website collector. The date
  filter runs **before** the `INGEST_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_SOURCE` cap, so yesterday
  is never used to fill the 50-post budget.

### Websites are added and removed from the panel

- HTMX `POST /api/ui/sources/website/save` and `.../{id}/deactivate`, plus a
  re-rendered list partial. Deactivation is the existing soft delete: post
  history and the idempotency keys survive.

### Fixed after the first live import (same day)

The first real import against the owner's account exposed three defects that no
mock had covered, all found by looking at the rendered page:

- **Avatars were never stored.** `download_media` returns `None` for an *entity*;
  a peer photo needs `download_profile_photo`. The import reported success and
  wrote 0 avatars. Now 35 of 38 rows render a real picture.
- **Private channels claimed to have no link.** They have no username, but a
  subscribed account resolves `t.me/c/<id>`, so 15 rows said "нет публичной
  ссылки" for channels the owner could in fact open. `_telegram_link` also
  rebuilds a URL from the stored identifier, so no row is left unclickable.
- **One peer showed as two rows.** `rian_ru` / `@rian_ru` and
  `@toporlive` / `-1001754252633` are the same channel under identifiers written
  by different import generations. `_matching_sources` matches on peer id and on
  the username with or without `@`; `_preferred_source` keeps the row holding
  collected posts (or the operator's tick), and the rest are retired — not
  deleted, because `raw_news.source_id` cascades. Retiring flushes *before* the
  keeper takes the canonical identifier, which is what the first attempt got
  wrong: both rows were renamed in one flush and PostgreSQL rejected it with
  `uq_sources_type_identifier`. That failure also surfaced as a bare 500, so the
  commit now reports a sentence instead.

### Fourth defect: rows outside the dialog list stayed bare

`tass_agency` and `interfaxonline` kept no kind, link, avatar or description, and
this was first written off as "the account is not subscribed to them". That was
wrong, and the owner said so. Checked instead of assumed: `get_entity` resolves
both as broadcast channels, so ingestion can read them — **Telegram lets a user
account read a public channel it never joined**, which means "absent from
`iter_dialogs`" says nothing about whether a source works.

- `MTProtoIngestor.describe_peer()` resolves one identifier and describes it in
  the same shape as a dialog.
- `_describe_unlisted_sources()` runs after the dialog union and fills in every
  row whose `peer_kind` is still NULL; an unresolvable identifier is skipped, so
  one dead entry cannot fail a refresh over dozens of rows.

Verified live on production after the fix: 42 rows, 3 retired duplicates,
37 avatars, 38 `t.me` links, 0 rows without a kind. ТАСС and Интерфакс now carry
`https://t.me/tass_agency` and `https://t.me/interfaxonline`, both with avatars.

### Verification

`ruff check .` clean, `mypy app scripts migrations/env.py` clean (78 files),
`pytest` 1147 tests passed, all 36 dashboard templates compile. Not verified:
live Telegram sign-in (QR / phone), and `scripts/smoke_test.py` — the local
server still has no `svodkapress` role. The migration itself **is** verified: it
ran against production PostgreSQL and the four columns exist. The final page was
confirmed by database state rather than by eye: the panel session had expired by
then.

## [2.10.0] — 2026-09-03 — Phase 0: durable article journals and clean-start safeguards

### Deployed to production 2026-09-03

Host `144.31.141.17`, directory `/opt/svodkapress`, copy deployment via `rsync`.

- Backups taken before any change: database dump
  `/opt/backups/svodkapress-20260903-154343-pre-0005.sql.gz` (gzip integrity
  verified, 9 tables) and previous code
  `/opt/backups/code-20260903-154700-pre-2.10.0.tar.gz`.
- `docker-compose.override.yml` deliberately excluded from the copy: it publishes
  PostgreSQL and Redis to the host, which must not happen on a public VPS.
- `scripts/deploy_vps.sh --check-only` passed, then the full run rebuilt the image,
  applied `0004_spec_alignment → 0005_article_journals`, and gated on `/health`
  and `/health/ready`.
- Verified live afterwards: version `2.10.0`; `article_journals` exists with both
  indexes, the unique `run_id` constraint and the `ON DELETE CASCADE` foreign key;
  data intact (292 articles, 1181 clusters, 1282 posts, 36 sources, 1 Telegram
  account); the journal screen renders; every listed article exposes its journal
  action; an anonymous journal request returns 401; a confirmed wipe attempt is
  refused with 409 because `APP_ENV=production`; no errors in app or worker logs.
- The three ARQ workers, manually stopped on 25 August, are running again.
- `pipeline_control.enabled` remains `false`, so no AI spend was triggered.

### Security finding — ports 8000 and 3005 are reachable from the internet

The `DOCKER-USER` chain is **empty**. `ufw` rules do not protect published Docker
ports because Docker's DNAT is evaluated before ufw's `INPUT`, so the existing
"allow only the proxy" rules have no effect on them. Confirmed from an external
address: TCP connects on both ports, and the application answers `400` only
because `ALLOWED_HOSTS` rejects the Host header. `docs/11_DEPLOYMENT_DEVOPS.md`
previously claimed this filtering was in place and persisted across reboots; that
claim was wrong and has been corrected. The two `iptables` rules that close it are
documented but **not applied**, because they change proxy reachability and need the
owner's confirmation.

**Resolved:** 8000 and 3005 closed in `DOCKER-USER` with rules persisted to
`/etc/iptables/rules.v4`. Proxy on 150.241.98.133/134 allowed, internal container
traffic and established connections returned, all other connections dropped. Tested
from external address: both ports now refused, panel via proxy still works.

### Full wipe

Old data (292 articles, 1181 clusters, 1282 posts) deleted on production per
owner's request. Backup taken before wipe:
`/opt/backups/svodkapress-20260903-163943-pre-wipe.sql.gz`. Confirmed no articles
had ever been published to WordPress (`wp_post_id` null across the table), so
nothing live was removed. LSH index in Redis already empty (TTL expired during
8-day idle period). Source watermarks cleared: all 36 sources ready for first pass.

### Dashboard redesign — macOS-style UI (partial deployment)

Templates rewritten for a native macOS aesthetic: sidebar navigation with window
lights, translucent sticky toolbar, SF-style typography, system blue as the sole
accent colour. The new design is built as reusable component classes (`.card`,
`.btn`, `.field`, `.chip`) loaded before Tailwind, with colours in CSS variables
instead of hardcoded. Twenty-seven pre-existing screens were not touched; instead
Tailwind's `slate`/`sky`/`emerald`/`rose`/`amber` scales are remapped via
`tailwind.config` to macOS greys and status colours, so every screen adopts the new
palette without markup changes.

Code backed up before sync:
`/opt/backups/code-20260903-175220-pre-redesign.tar.gz`. Application restarted,
health checks pass. **Visual verification deferred**: login screen renders with the
new palette, but full manual review of all screens requires entry and has not been
performed. No interactive elements were changed, HTMX targets are intact, and all
34 templates compile. Documented in `docs/DESIGN.md`.

### Added

- `article_journals` plus Alembic migration `0005_article_journals`: a small
  persistent index from each produced article to its run artifacts. It has a
  unique `run_id`, cluster traceability, chronological indexes, and cascades when
  the article is deleted.
- The content worker records a production journal after an article has been saved.
  Prompts and verbose stage details stay in `storage/runs/<run_id>/`; the database
  only carries the relationship and audit metadata.
- The articles table now exposes a **«Журнал»** action. It opens a protected HTMX
  timeline that shows stages, models, prompts, warnings and failures; a missing or
  corrupted artifact degrades to an honest notice instead of an error page.
- A full-clean-start preview and confirmation flow in the Laboratory. It identifies
  the database and host, reports affected content, preserves source/configuration
  tables, requires confirmation, and refuses `production`, remote PostgreSQL hosts,
  and database names without `_verify`, `_test`, `_scratch` or `_ci`.
- `RemoteTarget`, a validated non-secret contract for a future WordPress SSH
  binding. It reuses the existing host-key-checked transport but opens no network
  connection and stores no private key material.

### Changed

- Artifact files are written through an atomic temporary-file replacement.
- Pruning now removes only old laboratory runs without an `article_id`; historical
  evidence for a real article is retained until an explicit clean-start wipe.
- The smoke test now covers the journal table and its cascade, when a correctly
  configured scratch PostgreSQL database is available.

### Verification

- `ruff check .` — passed.
- `mypy app scripts migrations/env.py` — passed across 76 source files.
- `pytest -q` — passed in full.
- `alembic heads` — `0005_article_journals` is the sole head.
- `scripts.smoke_test` and `alembic check` could not connect to the local scratch
  database: PostgreSQL on `localhost:5432` rejected the configured role
  `svodkapress`. No rows, migrations, containers, VPS services or external systems
  were changed as part of that failed connection attempt.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html). Per `AGENTS.md`, one entry is appended per completed Package.

## [2.0.0] — 2026-08-23 — SvodkaPress NextGen: the complete autonomous pipeline

All five packages from `ROADMAP.md` are implemented, type-checked under `mypy` and
covered by **829 tests that require no external service**. A news item now travels
from a Telegram channel or a scraped page to a published WordPress article
distributed across four social platforms, with an operator dashboard over the whole
run.

The major version reflects a breaking change in shape rather than in API: 1.0.0 was
infrastructure and a schema, 2.0.0 is a working pipeline with five new ARQ workers, a
dashboard, and configuration that a 1.0.0 deployment does not have
(`ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH` and `FERNET_KEY` are now required to boot production).

### Pipeline, end to end

| Stage | Module | Cost |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ingest | `telegram_ingestor`, `web_scraper` | 0 tokens |
| Filter | `text_utils` heuristics | 0 tokens |
| Dedup L1 | `deduplicator` MinHash LSH in Redis | 0 tokens |
| Dedup L2/L3 | pgvector cosine + LLM arbiter | embeddings + arbiter only on the ambiguous band |
| Generate | `content_generator`, 20 editorial voices | 1 writer call, 1 corrective retry max |
| QA | `content_fixer` | 0 tokens unless a defect is found |
| Media | `watermark_detector` → crop, else `flux_generator` | OCR free; a cover only when the source is unusable |
| Publish | `wp_publisher` + `wp_post_formatter` | — |
| Distribute | Telegram, VK, OK, Dzen adapters | — |
| Measure | `seo_analytics` | — |

The zero-token stages are load-bearing: level 1 dedup rejects the bulk of a
republishing feed without touching an API, which is what makes the AI budget survive
a busy news day.

### Verification at release

- `pytest tests/` — **829 passed**, no external services
  (59 SEO, 60 dashboard, 34 publisher worker, 93 social, 89 wp publisher,
  69 wp media/vk, 66 media, 48 fixer, 112 content, 33 content worker,
  83 ingestion, 83 dedup)
- `ruff check .` — all checks passed
- `mypy app scripts migrations/env.py` — success, 57 source files
- `bash -n scripts/deploy_vps.sh` — valid; `--check-only` exercised against five
  `.env` states (missing, placeholder secret, short secret, bad password hash, valid)
- `docker-compose.yml` and `docker-compose.prod.yml` — both parse and merge

### Not verified

No live third-party account has been touched. GramJS, OpenRouter, Fal AI, VK, OK,
Dzen, Telegram, WordPress, Metrika and Search Console are each covered against a
mocked transport, which verifies our handling of the documented contract and not the
contract itself. First deployment should expect to adjust field names — Dzen's
partner-gated publishing API and Metrika's reporting fields are the likeliest to
differ. The dashboard templates and the SSE client have not been loaded in a browser.

### Deliberately unfinished

Three items need a product decision, not more code, and are listed under "Known
gaps" in `TODO.md`: saving article edits and executing the soft SEO optimisation
(both hinge on what a headline change does to an already-published post), writing API
keys through the settings UI, and per-platform enable/disable toggles.

---

### Added — Package 5, Micro-step 5.2: SEO watcher, DevOps finalisation & VPS deployment

Work performed 2026-08-23, ~21:05 to ~22:40 (UTC+03:00). **Completes Package 5.**

- `app/services/seo_analytics.py` — Yandex Metrika and Search Console clients plus
  the CTR/position watcher. `collect_seo_report()` joins both sources on the URL
  path and returns a value-ranked optimisation queue; `needs_optimisation()` and
  `build_optimisation_queue()` are exposed separately so the algorithm is testable
  without a network
- `app/api/dashboard.py`, `app/templates/dashboard/seo.html` — the `/seo` page with
  search-performance cards, the optimisation queue, the landing-page table, and the
  `Trigger Soft SEO Optimization` button wired to a new `/api/ui/seo/optimise`
  partial
- `scripts/deploy_vps.sh` — VPS deployment. Validates prerequisites, `.env`
  contents, secret lengths and both compose files **before** touching a running
  stack, then builds, migrates and gates on `/health` and `/health/ready`.
  `--check-only` runs every validation and changes nothing
- `scripts/remote_ssh.py` — `RemoteExecutor` for the WordPress host: public-key
  auth only, host-key verification on by default, `shlex`-quoted argument lists,
  SFTP upload, and a `reload_nginx()` that tests the config before reloading
- `tests/test_seo_analytics.py` — 59 tests

### Fixed — Package 5, Micro-step 5.2

- One of my own test defects: `test_the_queue_is_bounded` built 60 pages a few
  impressions apart, which round to identical `potential_clicks`, so their relative
  order is arbitrary and the assertion was flaky by construction. The fixture now
  spaces impressions widely enough that the ranking is unambiguous

### Security — Package 5, Micro-step 5.2

- **`deploy_vps.sh` reads `.env` with `sed`, never `source`.** Sourcing the file
  executes it, so a password containing a backtick or `$(...)` would run as a shell
  command with the deploying user's privileges
- **Validation runs before the build.** A deployment that would fail on a placeholder
  `SECRET_KEY` fails while the previous version is still serving, rather than after
  it has been replaced. The script rejects every `.env.example` placeholder, a
  `SECRET_KEY` under 32 characters, a non-bcrypt `ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH` and a
  malformed `FERNET_KEY`
- **SSH commands are argument lists, quoted individually with `shlex.quote`.** Five
  parametrised tests confirm that `; rm -rf /`, `$(whoami)`, backticks, `&& curl … |
  sh` and an embedded newline all survive as one inert argument. The raw-string form
  is named `run_shell` so choosing it is a visible decision
- **Host-key verification is on by default** and can only be disabled through a flag
  named `insecure_skip_host_key_check`, which logs a warning. Accepting any host key
  silently is how a deployment script hands its commands to whoever answers on port 22
- Password authentication is unsupported: a missing key is fatal rather than falling
  back to a prompt that would hang a deployment
- `nginx -t && systemctl reload nginx` are chained in one command, so a reload can
  never run after a failed config test — reloading a broken config takes the site down
- Remote output is truncated at 64 KB so a command that prints a log file cannot
  exhaust memory on a 4 GB VPS

### Notes — Package 5, Micro-step 5.2

- **CTR is judged against a position-aware expectation, not a flat 3%.** A 3% CTR at
  position 4 is a failure; the same 3% at position 15 is normal. A single flat cut-off
  would flood the queue with deep-ranking pages performing exactly as expected and
  bury the genuinely broken snippets. `EXPECTED_CTR_BY_POSITION` encodes the observed
  curve and is interpolated, because an average position is a real number. The
  requirement's 3% is kept as an absolute ceiling on top
- **The queue ranks by potential clicks, not by shortfall percentage.** A page missing
  90% of its expected clicks on 60 impressions is a worse use of the regeneration
  budget than one missing 40% on 20 000. A test asserts the ordering explicitly
- Positions 4-15 is the actionable band by construction: above it there is no
  headroom, below it a better snippet cannot help because the page is not seen
- Pages under 50 impressions are not judged. A URL with nine impressions and no
  clicks has a 0% CTR that means nothing, and regenerating on that signal spends
  tokens on noise
- The reporting window ends **yesterday**: both APIs finalise a day's data after it
  closes, so including today would report a partial figure and make every fresh
  article look like it is underperforming
- Both analytics sources degrade to warnings rather than raising. The SEO page is
  where an operator goes to discover that a token expired, so it must render when one
  has
- `GSCClient` accepts an OAuth access token rather than minting one from a service
  account: the JWT exchange needs `google-auth`, which the project does not carry, and
  accepting a token keeps the parsing — the part worth testing — independent of the
  credential flow
- Metrika's reporting field names are implemented against the documented
  `stat/v1/data` contract but **could not be verified against live docs** during this
  work (the documentation host returned 404). The parser is defensive about every
  field for that reason
- The two sources are joined on the URL **path**, the only key they agree on: GSC
  reports an absolute URL, Metrika a bare path. GSC is the authoritative side, so a
  page with no Metrika row is kept with zeroed behaviour metrics rather than dropped

### Added — Package 3, Micro-step 3.1: content generator, editorial categories & slop blocklist

Work performed 2026-08-23, ~11:40 to ~13:10 (UTC+03:00).

- `app/services/slop_filter.py` — the anti-cliché gate required by `AGENTS.md`. 40 rules split by severity: 30 `FATAL` (openers such as "В современном мире", tour-guide narration such as "Погрузимся в детали", summary closers such as "Подводя итог", consultant vocabulary such as "является ключевым фактором", plus the English register — `delve into`, `testament to`, `in conclusion`) and 10 `SOFT` repairable hedges. `validate_ai_text(text) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]` returns validity and the offending phrases as written; `clean_ai_slop(text)` strips only the repairable ones and tidies the seam (orphaned commas, doubled spaces, the lowercase letter left where a hedge was cut). Every rule is a regex over a normalised form, because Russian inflection defeats a literal blocklist: "является ключевым фактором" and "являются ключевыми факторами" are one cliché. Scoring is a density per 1000 words, so a short note and a longread face the same standard
- `app/services/content_prompts.py` — 20 editorial categories, each with its own voice, lead rule, structure and beat-specific prohibition (`proisshestviya` forbids speculation about causes before official findings; `zdorove-i-medicina` forbids dosages and diagnoses; `kriminal-i-pravo` enforces the presumption of innocence). `build_messages` assembles the `[system, user]` pair; `RESPONSE_JSON_SCHEMA` pins the seven output fields with the limits the database and Rank Math enforce
- `app/services/content_generator.py` — `generate_article(cluster_data, category, model_name=None)`. Validates shape then style, and retries **once** with the specific defects quoted back as a conversation turn. Returns a plain `dict`; the worker owns the transaction
- `app/services/ai_client.py` — new `complete_json()` for multi-turn completions with a `response_format` schema and a caller-supplied token budget
- `tests/test_content_engine.py` — 112 tests against a `respx`-mocked API: dirty-vs-clean detection, per-pattern-family coverage, prompt construction for all 20 categories, response-schema validation, and the retry paths

### Fixed — Package 3, Micro-step 3.1

- `slop:new_level` did not match any real sentence: the pattern required "вывело" and "на новый уровень" to be adjacent, while the idiom is split by its object ("вывело **отрасль** на новый уровень"). Now allows up to four intervening words, bounded so two clauses cannot match across a comma. The verb stem also changes across tenses (вывод-/вывел-/вывед-), which one shared tail could not cover
- Nested rules double-counted one cliché: "стоит отметить, что" also matched the bare "стоит отметить,", inflating the density that decides validity and wrongly rejecting a draft with a single hedge. Overlapping occurrences are now suppressed in favour of the most specific match

### Security — Package 3, Micro-step 3.1

- Generated `content_html` is **rejected**, not sanitised, when it carries `<script>`, `<style>`, `<iframe>`, `<object>`, `<embed>`, `<form>`, document tags, inline `on*` handlers or `style` attributes. A script in a generated article means the model echoed markup out of a scraped source, and silently stripping it would hide an attempted prompt injection that an operator needs to see
- A deployment without `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` raises `NoCredentialsError` here rather than degrading: unlike deduplication, content generation has no zero-token fallback, and a silent skip would leave clusters permanently unpublished
- The retry budget is fixed at two attempts. Escalating retries would turn one stubborn cluster into an unbounded bill

### Added — Package 5, Micro-step 5.1: dashboard, SSE logs, articles editor & sources manager

Work performed 2026-08-23, ~19:15 to ~21:00 (UTC+03:00).

- `app/core/security.py` — bcrypt password hashing (cost 12, used directly rather than through the unmaintained `passlib`), JWT session issue/verify with the algorithm **pinned** to the configured one, and a Fernet `SecretBox` for API keys at rest. Ships a CLI: `python -m app.core.security hash <password>` and `genkey`
- `app/api/auth.py` — `POST /auth/login`, `POST /auth/logout`, `GET /auth/me`. The session is a JWT in an `HttpOnly` + `SameSite=Lax` cookie, `Secure` in production. Two guards on purpose: `get_current_admin_user` returns 401 for API callers and HTMX partials, `require_admin_page` returns a 303 redirect for browsers
- `app/api/logs_sse.py` — `GET /api/logs/stream` relaying the Redis `pipeline:events` channel as Server-Sent Events, plus `RedisLogHandler` for the worker processes. The handler never raises, never blocks and drops records rather than queueing without bound
- `app/api/dashboard.py` + `app/templates/dashboard/` — seven templates (base, login, index, articles, sources, settings, logs) and three HTMX partials. Tailwind and HTMX from pinned CDN tags, so there is no node_modules build step
- `app/config.py`, `.env.example` — `ADMIN_USERNAME`, `ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH`, `SESSION_COOKIE_NAME`, `FERNET_KEY`. The production validator now refuses to boot without a password hash or a Fernet key
- `tests/test_dashboard_ui.py` — 60 tests driving the real ASGI app through `httpx.ASGITransport`, with the database dependency overridden and the lifespan deliberately not run

### Fixed — Package 5, Micro-step 5.1

- **The SSE heartbeat could never fire.** The idle branch depended on `asyncio.wait_for` raising `TimeoutError` around `pubsub.get_message`, but `get_message` returns `None` *immediately* when the queue is empty, so the timeout was unreachable and an idle stream sat silent forever — every log connection would have been closed by the first proxy in the path, and the terminal would have appeared broken whenever the pipeline was quiet. The idle interval is now tracked explicitly against the loop clock
- **The same read loop busy-spun.** Because `get_message` returns instantly, `continue`ing straight to the next call starved the event loop — including the publisher trying to deliver the very event the stream was waiting for. Found as a hang while verifying the stream by hand. Fixed with a short yield; without it the endpoint would have pinned a CPU core per connected browser
- Three defects in my own tests, each of which had been asserting the wrong thing:
  - The admin-credential fixture patched the *class* attribute of the frozen `Settings`, which pydantic silently ignores because the instance's `__dict__` shadows it. Every authenticated test was failing. `object.__setattr__` on the instance is what takes effect
  - The XSS assertion searched for the substring `onerror=alert(1)`, which legitimately survives as inert text inside a quoted, escaped attribute. It now asserts the property that matters — that no unescaped `<` reaches the page
  - The heartbeat test cancelled the consuming task, which discarded the frames it had collected; and the pub/sub test read the channel once after a fixed sleep, which is a race. Both now drive the generator directly and poll

### Security — Package 5, Micro-step 5.1

- **This replaces the interim shared-secret bearer guard** that `app/api/ingestion.py` flagged as a Package 5 placeholder. Those endpoints repoint the pipeline and start collection runs, so they were never safe to leave open
- Each cookie flag is asserted by a test: `HttpOnly` (an XSS anywhere on the dashboard cannot read the token — the single most valuable flag), `SameSite=Lax` (a cross-site POST cannot carry the session, which is what makes CSRF against the mutating endpoints impractical), `Secure` in production, and `Path=/` so one `delete_cookie` actually clears it
- **The login form is not an open redirect.** `?next=` is accepted only as a plain site-relative path; `https://evil.test`, `//evil.test`, and `/\evil.test` all fall back to `/`. Without the check an attacker sends a login link and the operator is handed to a convincing clone *after* authenticating. Four parametrised tests cover it
- The JWT algorithm is pinned rather than read from the token header, which is the classic confusion attack (re-sign with `none`, or downgrade RS256 to HS256 using the public key as the HMAC secret). A token signed correctly but for a *different* subject is also rejected, so renaming the administrator invalidates old sessions
- Login failures are vague in both content and timing: one message for both failure modes, and the password is verified even when the username is wrong, so response time does not reveal which half matched
- API keys are **masked** on the settings page — last four characters only. A test asserts the plaintext is absent from the rendered HTML, because a credential in a page can be screenshotted, cached by a proxy or captured in a session recording
- The article body is rendered as escaped source in a `readonly` textarea, never as live markup. It is model output that quoted scraped pages, and the admin panel must not be the one place where it finally executes
- Every dashboard page carries `noindex, nofollow`
- The Fernet key is separate from `SECRET_KEY` by design: rotating the JWT signing key should log everyone out, not render every stored API key permanently undecryptable
